The nose behind this fragrance is Antoine Maisondieu. Top notes are Citron, Lemon and Bergamot; middle notes are Magnolia, Jasmine, Rose and Tuberose; base notes are Vetiver, Sandalwood, Patchouli and Vanilla. </p><div v-pre="" class="fragrantica-blockquote"><p>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile continues tradition of classical fragrances of this house. The perfume arrives in July 2009, in a flacon shaped like flacon of Iris Nobile. It incorporates essences of bergamot, citruses, cedar, magnolia, Bulgarian rose, jasmine, sandalwood, patchouli, vanilla and vetiver. The fragrance is available as 50 and 100ml edp. Perfumer is Antoine Maisondieu.</p></div><p class="reviewstrigger" style="font-size: smaller; color: rgb(131, 166, 196);">Read about this perfume in other languages: <a href="https://www.fragrantica.de/Parfum/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Deutsch</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.es/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Español</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.fr/parfum/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Français</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.cz/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Čeština</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.it/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Italiano</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.ru/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Русский</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.pl/perfumy/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Polski</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.com.br/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/

Perfumer is Antoine Maisondieu.</p></div><p class="reviewstrigger" style="font-size: smaller; color: rgb(131, 166, 196);">Read about this perfume in other languages: <a href="https://www.fragrantica.de/Parfum/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Deutsch</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.es/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Español</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.fr/parfum/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Français</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.cz/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Čeština</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.it/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Italiano</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.ru/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Русский</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.pl/perfumy/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Polski</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.com.br/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Português</a>, <a href="h

The nose behind this fragrance is Antoine Maisondieu. Top notes are Citron, Lemon and Bergamot; middle notes are Magnolia, Jasmine, Rose and Tuberose; base notes are Vetiver, Sandalwood, Patchouli and Vanilla. </p><div v-pre="" class="fragrantica-blockquote"><p>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile continues tradition of classical fragrances of this house. The perfume arrives in July 2009, in a flacon shaped like flacon of Iris Nobile. It incorporates essences of bergamot, citruses, cedar, magnolia, Bulgarian rose, jasmine, sandalwood, patchouli, vanilla and vetiver. The fragrance is available as 50 and 100ml edp. Perfumer is Antoine Maisondieu.</p></div><p class="reviewstrigger" style="font-size: smaller; color: rgb(131, 166, 196);">Read about this perfume in other languages: <a href="https://www.fragrantica.de/Parfum/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Deutsch</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.es/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Español</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.fr/parfum/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Français</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.cz/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Čeština</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.it/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" targe

</div></div></div><div class="small-12 medium-6 text-center" style="max-height: 50px;"><div itemprop="aggregateRating" itemtype="http://schema.org/AggregateRating" itemscope=""><p class="info-note">Perfume rating <span itemprop="ratingValue">4.11</span> out of <span itemprop="bestRating">5</span> with <span itemprop="ratingCount" content="2581">2,581</span> votes<meta itemprop="reviewCount" content="158"></p></div></div></div><div class="grid-x grid-margin-x grid-margin-y"><div class="cell small-12" itemprop="description"><p><b>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile</b> by <b>Acqua di Parma</b> is a Floral Fruity fragrance for women. <b>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile</b> was launched in 2009. The nose behind this fragrance is Antoine Maisondieu. Top notes are Citron, Lemon and Bergamot; middle notes are Magnolia, Jasmine, Rose and Tuberose; base notes are Vetiver, Sandalwood, Patchouli and Vanilla. </p><div v-pre="" class="fragrantica-blockquote"><p>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile continues tradition of classical fragrances of this house. The perfume arrives in July 2009, in a flacon shaped like flacon of Iris Nobile. It incorporates essences of bergamot, citruses, cedar, magnolia, Bulgarian rose, jasmine, sandalwood, patchouli, vanilla and vetiver. The fragrance is available as 50 and 100ml edp. Perfumer is Antoine Maisondieu.</p></div><p class="reviewstrigger" style="font-size: smaller; color: rgb(131, 166, 196);">Read about this perfume in other languages: <a href="https://www.fragrantica.de/Parfum/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Deutsch</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.es/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Español</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.fr/parfum/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Français</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.cz/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Čeština</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.it/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Italiano</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.ru/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Русский</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.pl/perfumy/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Polski</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.com.br/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobi

div></div></div></div></div><div class="small-12 medium-6 text-center" style="max-height: 50px;"><div itemprop="aggregateRating" itemtype="http://schema.org/AggregateRating" itemscope=""><p class="info-note">Perfume rating <span itemprop="ratingValue">4.11</span> out of <span itemprop="bestRating">5</span> with <span itemprop="ratingCount" content="2581">2,581</span> votes<meta itemprop="reviewCount" content="158"></p></div></div></div><div class="grid-x grid-margin-x grid-margin-y"><div class="cell small-12" itemprop="description"><p><b>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile</b> by <b>Acqua di Parma</b> is a Floral Fruity fragrance for women. <b>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile</b> was launched in 2009. The nose behind this fragrance is Antoine Maisondieu. Top notes are Citron, Lemon and Bergamot; middle notes are Magnolia, Jasmine, Rose and Tuberose; base notes are Vetiver, Sandalwood, Patchouli and Vanilla. </p><div v-pre="" class="fragrantica-blockquote"><p>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile continues tradition of classical fragrances of this house. The nose behind this fragrance is Antoine Maisondieu. Top notes are Citron, Lemon and Bergamot; middle notes are Magnolia, Jasmine, Rose and Tuberose; base notes are Vetiver, Sandalwood, Patchouli and Vanilla. </p><div v-pre="" class="fragrantica-blockquote"><p>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile continues tradition of classical fragrances of this house. The perfume arrives in July 2009, in a flacon shaped like flacon of Iris Nobile. It incorporates essences of bergamot, citruses, cedar, magnolia, Bulgarian rose, jasmine, sandalwood, patchouli, vanilla and vetiver. The fragrance is available as 50 and 100ml edp. Perfumer is Antoine Maisondieu.</p></div><p class="reviewstrigger" style="font-size: smaller; color: rgb(131, 166, 196);">Read about this perfume in other languages: <a href="https://www.fragrantica.de/Parfum/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Deutsch</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.es/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Español</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.fr/parfum/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Français</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.cz/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Čeština</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.it/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Italiano</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.ru/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Русский</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.pl/perfumy/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Polski</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.com.br/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-P

="anonymous" referrerpolicy="no-referrer"></script><script src="/new-js/mfga-app.js?v=rtg5Ffdd2f6da36b5b20642efa5a90ffb033c"></script><div class="tooltip" role="tooltip" aria-hidden="true" data-is-active="false" data-is-focus="false" id="0p427e-tooltip" style="display: none;">I've been debating for quite some time whether to buy this or not, and I finally gave in. "158"></p></div></div></div><div class="grid-x grid-margin-x grid-margin-y"><div class="cell small-12" itemprop="description"><p><b>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile</b> by <b>Acqua di Parma</b> is a Floral Fruity fragrance for women. <b>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile</b> was launched in 2009. The nose behind this fragrance is Antoine Maisondieu. Top notes are Citron, Lemon and Bergamot; middle notes are Magnolia, Jasmine, Rose and Tuberose; base notes are Vetiver, Sandalwood, Patchouli and Vanilla. </p><div v-pre="" class="fragrantica-blockquote"><p>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile continues tradition of classical fragrances of this house. The perfume arrives in July 2009, in a flacon shaped like flacon of Iris Nobile. It incorporates essences of bergamot, citruses, cedar, magnolia, Bulgarian rose, jasmine, sandalwood, patchouli, vanilla and vetiver. The fragrance is available as 50 and 100ml edp. Perfumer is Antoine Maisondieu.</p></div><p class="reviewstrigger" style="font-size: smaller; color: rgb(131, 166, 196);">Read about this perfume in other languages: <a href="https://www.fragrantica.de/Parfum/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Deutsch</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.es/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Español</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.fr/parfum/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Français</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.cz/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Čeština</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.it/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Italiano</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.ru/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Русский</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.pl/perfumy/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Polski</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.com.br/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Português</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.gr/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Ελληνικά</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.asia/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">汉语</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.nl/parfum/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-M

Top notes are Citron, Lemon and Bergamot; middle notes are Magnolia, Jasmine, Rose and Tuberose; base notes are Vetiver, Sandalwood, Patchouli and Vanilla. </p><div v-pre="" class="fragrantica-blockquote"><p>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile continues tradition of classical fragrances of this house. The perfume arrives in July 2009, in a flacon shaped like flacon of Iris Nobile. It incorporates essences of bergamot, citruses, cedar, magnolia, Bulgarian rose, jasmine, sandalwood, patchouli, vanilla and vetiver. The fragrance is available as 50 and 100ml edp. ## `Vetiver`
- hits: **4**

```text
50px;"><div itemprop="aggregateRating" itemtype="http://schema.org/AggregateRating" itemscope=""><p class="info-note">Perfume rating <span itemprop="ratingValue">4.11</span> out of <span itemprop="bestRating">5</span> with <span itemprop="ratingCount" content="2581">2,581</span> votes<meta itemprop="reviewCount" content="158"></p></div></div></div><div class="grid-x grid-margin-x grid-margin-y"><div class="cell small-12" itemprop="description"><p><b>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile</b> by <b>Acqua di Parma</b> is a Floral Fruity fragrance for women. <b>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile</b> was launched in 2009. The nose behind this fragrance is Antoine Maisondieu. Top notes are Citron, Lemon and Bergamot; middle notes are Magnolia, Jasmine, Rose and Tuberose; base notes are Vetiver, Sandalwood, Patchouli and Vanilla. </p><div v-pre="" class="fragrantica-blockquote"><p>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile continues tradition of classical fragrances of this house. The perfume arrives in July 2009, in a flacon shaped like flacon of Iris Nobile. It incorporates essences of bergamot, citruses, cedar, magnolia, Bulgarian rose, jasmine, sandalwood, patchouli, vanilla and vetiver. The fragrance is available as 50 and 100ml edp. d-margin-x grid-margin-y"><div class="cell small-12" itemprop="description"><p><b>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile</b> by <b>Acqua di Parma</b> is a Floral Fruity fragrance for women. <b>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile</b> was launched in 2009. The nose behind this fragrance is Antoine Maisondieu. Top notes are Citron, Lemon and Bergamot; middle notes are Magnolia, Jasmine, Rose and Tuberose; base notes are Vetiver, Sandalwood, Patchouli and Vanilla. </p><div v-pre="" class="fragrantica-blockquote"><p>Acqua di Parma Magnolia Nobile continues tradition of classical fragrances of this house. The perfume arrives in July 2009, in a flacon shaped like flacon of Iris Nobile. It incorporates essences of bergamot, citruses, cedar, magnolia, Bulgarian rose, jasmine, sandalwood, patchouli, vanilla and vetiver. The fragrance is available as 50 and 100ml edp. Perfumer is Antoine Maisondieu.</p></div><p class="reviewstrigger" style="font-size: smaller; color: rgb(131, 166, 196);">Read about this perfume in other languages: <a href="https://www.fragrantica.de/Parfum/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Deutsch</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.es/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Español</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.fr/parfum/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Français</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.cz/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Čeština</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.it/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Italiano</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.ru/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Русский</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.pl/perfumy/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Polski</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.com.br/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Português</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.gr/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Ελληνικά</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.asia/perfume/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">汉语</a>, <a href="https://www.fragrantica.nl/parfum/Acqua-di-Parma/Acqua-di-Parma-Magnolia-Nobile-6483.html" target="_blank" rel="noo

The nose behind this fragrance is Quentin Bisch. Top notes are Lavender and Mint; middle notes are Vanilla and Benzoin; base notes are Honey, Tonka Bean and Tobacco. </p> <div class="fragrantica-blockquote" v-pre=""> <p> Welcome on board! <br/> The new sailor Le Male Elixir will induce burning cravings and drive your senses crazy. Burning desire spreads with the irresistible notes of fresh lavender, warming tonka bean, and gentle benzoin. With a gilded torso, this fiery novelty from Jean Paul Gaultier embodies radiant power and seductive charisma. <br/> It is finished! You melt away with burning desire. </p> <p> This intense fragrance ignites the fire of the senses on his upper body and gives the soft skin a burning sensuality. After a refreshing breeze of mint, the tide rises to addiction, becoming a glowing elixir. In this breath of fresh air, dashing lavender quickly takes on lusty proportions, enhanced by the balsamic richness of benzoin with syrupy vanilla accents. The tonka bean trumps with its warm and sweet tones between honey and tobacco. The time has come: You jump! </p> <p> Radiant at first sight, glowing with masculinity, more fiery than ever. The torso is bursting with muscles, the skin is bathed in copper color by the sinking sun. What a Gaultier body... what accomplished style. Finely chiseled grooves stand out on its muscles dipped in gold and merge with the amber stripes of the sailor shirt, which plays with transparency effects in a highly sexy way. </p> <p> Available as 75ml and 125ml Parfum. </p> </div> <p class="reviewstrigger" style="font-si
```

<span content="21243" itemprop="ratingCount"> 21,243 </span> votes <meta content="2087" itemprop="reviewCount"/> </p> </div> </div> </div> <div class="grid-x grid-margin-x grid-margin-y"> <div class="cell small-12" itemprop="description" v-pre=""> <p> <b> Le Male Elixir </b> by <b> Jean Paul Gaultier </b> is a Oriental Fougere fragrance for men. <b> Le Male Elixir </b> was launched in 2023. The nose behind this fragrance is Quentin Bisch. Top notes are Lavender and Mint; middle notes are Vanilla and Benzoin; base notes are Honey, Tonka Bean and Tobacco. </p> <div class="fragrantica-blockquote" v-pre=""> <p> Welcome on board! <br/> The new sailor Le Male Elixir will induce burning cravings and drive your senses crazy. Burning desire spreads with the irresistible notes of fresh lavender, warming tonka bean, and gentle benzoin. With a gilded torso, this fiery novelty from Jean Paul Gaultier embodies radiant power and seductive charisma. <br/> It is finished! You melt away with burning desire. </p> <p> This intense fragrance ignites the fire of the senses on his upper body and gives the soft skin a burning sensuality. After a refreshing breeze of mint, the tide rises to addiction, becoming a glowing elixir. In this breath of fresh air, dashing lavender quickly takes on lusty proportions, enhanced by the balsamic richness of benzoin with syrupy vanilla accents. The tonka bean trumps with its warm and sweet tones between honey and tobacco. The time has come: You jump! </p> <p> Radiant at first sight, glowing with masculinity, more fiery than ever. The torso is bursting with muscles, the skin is bathed in copper color by the sinking sun. What a Gaultier body... what accomplished style. Finely chiseled grooves stand out on its muscles dipped in gold and merge with the amber stripes of the sailor shirt, which plays with transparency effects in a highly sexy way. </p> <p> Available as 75ml and 125ml Parfum. </p> </div> <p class="reviewstrigger" style="font-size: smaller; color: #83a6c4"> Rea

```text
ground: rgba(250,247,236,0.76); padding: 0.2rem;" v-sticky=""> <p> <b class="idLinkify" title="https://www.fragrantica.com/member/2215607"> Theonewhosmellsgoodaf </b> <span class="vote-button-legend" content="2025-12-19" itemprop="datePublished" style="padding: 0 1rem;"> 12/19/25 07:37 </span> </p> </div> <div class="flex-child-auto" style="position: relative;"> <div itemprop="reviewBody" v-pre=""> <p> This is the best Le Male fragrance so far imo. It starts out relatively fresh like the OG Le Male, purely based of mint and lavender with vanilla in the background. When it dries down though, it gets more sirupy sweet. Honey becomes more prominent, complimented by vanilla and benzoin. The tobacco isn’t so noticeable, but it is blended very well with the vanillin/tonka bean. Basically yummy vanilla with a touch of tobacco. <br> <br> People usually compare this to the Le Parfum version. When i first smelled this, it immediately reminded me of Le Parfum. If you smell them side by side, they are completely different. They share the same Le Male DNA, and the same vanillin base. The difference is that Le Parfum is more powdery and spicy, while Le Male Elixir is more on the sirupy sweet side, and ofc way more linear. Le Male Elixir’s longevity is beastly, way better than Le Parfum’s longevity. <br> <br> Longevity: 10/10 <br> Projection: 10/10 <br> Scent Profile: 8.75/10 <br> Compliment factor: 7.25/10 <br> Overall: 9/10. Highly Recommended, but not blind buy safe. Smell it before buy it. </br> </br> </br> </br> </br> </br> </br> </br> </p> </div> </div> <div class="flex-child-shrink"> <div style="display:flex; align-items: baseline;"> <vote-buttons comment_id="
```
